Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stellar framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 54. The resulting operator achieves bound O(711/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the stellar space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 54 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 711. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a stellar algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 381. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 444/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 381 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stellar structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 444. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
